Reads: `loadSnapshot()` (V12), `loadBench()` merged with observed (V11), live `Providers` via `rpc("getConfig", [], {timeoutMs: 5000})` then the library's ONE resolvability rule (cr-m7: provider found with `enabled !== false` AND `models.includes(rest)`; `routableFromConfig` ignores `enabled`, V23, so it is NOT reused as is and its picker behaviour is left unchanged), `default-model.load()`, the six alias env values read-only from settings (for `exempt` and `ctxHints`), the vault registry and key choices read-only (`~/.llmkeys/registry.json`, `key-choices.json`: the key TIER per provider for the free scopes, D-k; ids, tiers and env var names only, never a secret, and nothing else in them is used) and, from S5, `state/tool-fidelity.json` read DIRECTLY (cr-M3: not through the bench loader and not through the snapshot). The CLI takes every one of these through a test-only `--*-file` flag (section 4), so fixtures never touch the real service, `~/.llmkeys` or `state/`. SECURITY: `getConfig` returns `api_key` values unredacted (`harness/config.mjs` comment `:84`); the compiler keeps only provider `name`, `models`, `enabled` and `modelDescriptions` presence, never stringifies or logs the config, and never opens `config.sqlite`.

Per snapshot row `r` and model `m` (a "route"), in order, each stage counted for the funnel:
1. Chat-capable: `m.mode !== true` AND (`m.outModality` in {`chat`, `chat?`} OR (`m.outModality == null` AND `m.outputKind !== "nontext"`)). (`chat?` is the modality module's word for "answered a bench chat probe, primary modality not proven", `menu/modality.mjs:5-15`; excluding it would drop 672 routes, 13 of them the Anthropic relay rows.) This excludes the 9 pool/ocr rows and image, audio, video, embed, rank, stt, moderation rows.
2. Selector: strip a trailing `[1m]`/`[1M]` to get the bare id; selector = `${r.provider}/${m.id}` if that exact string is in the live `Providers` set, else the bare form if that is, else the route is dropped (stage "in-providers"). `x` and `x[1m]` collapse into one selector; effective ctx is the max over the collapsed routes, 1M-basis flag from either; `n = 1` when the 1M claim comes only from a `[1m]` sibling or a relay row. The selector then passes the id sanitiser (section 5.2, cr-m3: charset and 64-character cap, raised to 160 by sa-A10); rejects are counted as `id-rejected` and listed, never silently dropped. A route whose live `Providers` spelling is ONLY `x[1m]` (no bare spelling listed) is NOT an `id-rejected` case: it is dropped with its own reason `only-1m-spelling-listed` and an `ONLY_1M_SPELLING` warning (revision 7, item 9).
3. Probe health: merged bench record `s === "ok"` and `isUsable` (V11). Records with `pay`, `auth`, `rate`, `timeout`, `error`, `empty`, `gone`, `skip` do not qualify but are counted and printed (responding-provider rule).
4. Tools: while no tool-fidelity record exists (S1-S4), `m.tools !== false` admits the route and it is tier `u` (UNVERIFIED) and COUNTED as unverified (D-f: allowed WITH a warning surfaced in `set`, `show`, the injected list, the standing doctor WARN and the status field). With S5, tiers come from `state/tool-fidelity.json` keyed by benchKey (cr-M3, section 11), and once a model HAS a probe result the catalogue `tools` claim (true or false) is ignored for it (D-i: the 77 `tools: false` models are probed too and judged by the probe): tier `x` (L1 or L2 failed, or L3 failed where it was run) excluded always (explicit pin needs `--force`), `v` (L3 passed at the recorded size, or known-good by provenance: the 13 Anthropic relay models, never probed) eligible, `t` (L1+L2 passed, L3 not run) and `u` (not probed or stale) eligible only under `unverified: allow-warn` or an `allow` pin, `t` printed as "tools work at small payload".
5. Toggle 2 `free`, by scope (D-j; names `free models`, `free providers`, `free providers + deposit`, D-l). PROVIDER TIER COMES FROM THE VAULT REGISTRY, NEVER FROM THE SNAPSHOT `keyId` (D-k, I11): the compiler reads `~/.llmkeys/registry.json` and `key-choices.json`, applies `filterRegistry` then `chooseKeys` (`keysync/keysync.mjs:60-110`) to find the key wired for each provider and takes that row `tier` (full-length ids, so the 30-character snapshot clip of issue #148 cannot affect it); the Anthropic relay is `subscription` by construction; a provider with no resolvable key has no tier. If the registry or key choices cannot be read, the scopes `providers` and `providers+deposit` REFUSE (exit 4, `E_TIER_UNREADABLE`, section 7 exit-code table) and never fall back to the snapshot key id (the scope `models` still compiles but applies the STRICT rule to every provider whose tier it cannot read, with a warning). Every scope first requires stages 1-4 (probe-ok per stage 3, never `pay` or any other non-ok status, responding-provider rule) and NO scope ever admits a `subscription` or `management` row.
   - `free models`: a model whose badge is `FREE` or `FREE?`, OR whose BARE id carries a free tag (I9, I10), on providers whose key tier is `free`; on `free-deposit` and `paid` providers the STRICT rule applies in THIS scope too (M5: only free-tagged rows are admitted, badge-only and price-only rows are skipped and counted `deposit-strict-skipped`); never on `subscription` or `management`; where the tier is unknown the strict rule applies (including a multi-key provider with no deliberate choice: this scope never exits `E_KEY_AMBIGUOUS`, it warns `KEY_AMBIGUOUS` / `NO_TIER`; the provider scopes below keep exit 4; a management-only provider is never admitted under any scope; revision 7, item 8). `FREE_TAG` is one named pattern tested on the BARE id (the id after the provider prefix with a trailing `[1m]`/`[1M]` stripped, AND on every collapsed sibling spelling: `x:free[1m]` does NOT match raw, M2), case-insensitive: `(^|[:/_.-])free($|[:/_.-])` (I9; matches `x:free`, `x-free`, `a/free/b`, `x_free`, `free-x`; never `freedom`, `freeform`, `free2`).
   - `free providers`: EVERY stage-1-4 row whose provider tier is `free` (a free label is not needed; the key tier is the signal).
   - `free providers + deposit`: `free providers` PLUS, inside providers whose tier is `free-deposit` or `paid`, ONLY rows whose id matches `FREE_TAG` (OWNER RULE, strict): every other probe-ok model on those providers is SKIPPED, including a model priced 0 and a model with a `FREE`/`FREE?` badge, because neither is a sufficient signal there; there is no per-provider override list. Skipped rows are counted as `deposit-strict-skipped` in the funnel and listed by `explain`. On the 2026-10-03 replay the tagged rows on paid-tier providers are 3 (two `gone`, one `pay`), so this scope equals `free providers` today.
   - Price: shown on every row. Under `mode free` with a provider scope, rank key 2b (section 5.3, revision 8 item 2) orders price-0 free-tier models first (class 0), then free-tagged rows on paid or deposit providers (class 0t), then unknown price, then positive prices cheapest first (credit conservation).
   - G17 (revision 8 item 3): a provider whose tier is `excluded` (`management`) is dropped at this stage for EVERY scope and contributes to no count, list, ranking, injected text or hash.
   - Funnel: the compiler computes ALL THREE scope counts (probe-ok models and providers, after the tools stage) on every compile and `set` and `show` print them whichever scope is chosen, so the owner sees what each scope would give before choosing (section 7).
   - Staleness: a live 402 flips a model to `pay` in the overlay; the compiled lists exclude it only after the next `rebuild` (section 7 `rebuild`, doctor `FREE_LIST_STALE`).
   - Pool aliases (D-n): a probe-ok row whose id matches the shared `POOL_ALIAS_RE` (single constant in `menu/pool-rule.mjs`, section 4; last id segment `auto`, `router`, `default` or `free`; documented STOP-GAP until a data-driven pool classification exists) is ADMITTED to a scope on the same terms as any other row (it can also match `FREE_TAG`, e.g. `kilo-auto/free`, or the `free` tier of its provider) but carries `al: 1` and the `ALIAS` flag in the funnel line (counted separately, `counts.alias`), `show`, `explain`, the injected row and the compiled policy. Its served model is unknown, so: (a) tool-fidelity results for an alias are recorded with `alias: true`, are ADVISORY, and the compiler caps an alias row at tier `u` whatever the probe said; (b) it ranks below non-alias rows at otherwise equal keys (rank key 5, section 5.3); (c) it is substitutable only if its catalogue context is known.
   - Tier cross-check (M-a): the snapshot is rebuilt with full-length key ids (V28), so the compiler also derives the tier segment of each row `keyId` and compares it with the registry-derived provider tier; a difference emits `TIER_MISMATCH` (warning, registry value used, never the snapshot value).
6. Toggle 3 1m: `ctx >= 1,000,000` OR the id ended in `[1m]`. `ctx: null` without the tag fails.
7. Toggle 1 scope: `all-providers` keeps everything; `same-provider` groups by provider into `lists.byProvider`; the router selects by main's provider at request time.
8. `inherit` computes no lists (main is the answer); the compiler still emits the funnel for `explain`, the `exempt` selectors (I8), and warns (a) if `ctx: 1m` and the likely main is below 1M (D-d: inherits anyway) and (b) if the likely or any learned main is PREMIUM (D-b).
9. Fit fields (cr-M1), computed for every allowed row: `c` (effective ctx; unknown is 0 and can never be a substitute because of the 128k floor) and `pb` (payload cap bytes from the snapshot's per-provider entry, V27; 0 unknown). The compiler also fills `ctxHints` for the asked-side floor (section 6.2) and counts `payloadRisk` (known `pb` under `PAYLOAD_RISK_BYTES` = 1,000,000) and `payloadUnknown`. These are a PRECONDITION for enforcement (G3): `set --enforce enforce` refuses if the compiled file lacks them.
10. Emptiness map (D-c, cr-M8): after the toggles are applied, `emptyProviders[]` = every provider with at least one chat-capable route present in live `Providers` whose SUBSTITUTE pool is empty (`substitutable = 0`, M3, section 5.2); `thinProviders[]` = pool of 1 or 2; `substitutable[provider]` is compiled for every provider (rows with a KNOWN context of at least 128,000 and a resolvable selector). `lists.byProvider` omits those providers, so this array is the only way to tell "empty" from "not computed"; it feeds the `set` warning, the picker note and `show`. `noRowProviders` (revision 8 item 1, S4b) is computed in the same pass: every non-excluded provider with at least one chat-capable route present in live `Providers` and ZERO rows in the candidate set; it is disjoint from `emptyProviders[]` and `[]` under `inherit`.
11. Premium flag (D-b): `p = 1` when the catalogue family is `opus` or `fable`, or the output price is at least $20 per M tokens (the named constant `PREMIUM_RULE`; owner-confirmed, Q3 closed). Price is shown on every row of every output; nothing is excluded by it.

Interplay with `default-model` and the six tier aliases. Nothing in keysync changes. The six alias env values (`opusModel`, `sonnetModel`, `haikuModel`, `smallFastModel`, `fableModel`, `model`) are what Claude Code puts in `body.model` for alias-based spawns, so they arrive at the router as `asked` and are judged like any other id (with the relay up they are `anthropic/...` ids; `docs/runbook.md:153-155` notes inheriting subagents get the default model). The default model file only sets `profile.model`, i.e. main's start model; the policy learns main from traffic, not from that file, and uses the file solely to guess "likely main" for the R2 refusal. The doctor asserts `CLAUDE_CODE_SUBAGENT_MODEL` is unset in settings `env` and that no `availableModels`/`deniedModels` exist (either would silently defeat or constrain the policy).

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

## 9. Test plan

Principle: the router is a pure-enough CommonJS function, so the whole algorithm is unit-tested against the REAL file; the sandbox exists only for what unit tests cannot prove about CCR (five integration facts). No test reads the real `~/.llmkeys`, `state/`, `service.json` or a live gateway; all use temp directories, the CLI is always run with the full fixture flag set from the helper (so `rpc()`, which reads `service.json` by default, V6, is never reached; revision 7 item 7 and revision 9 item 2: fixture mode is on when ANY file flag is present, a required set of flags must then be given together, an optional flag not given resolves under `<state>/_not-given/`, and the helper itself refuses any directory outside `os.tmpdir()` that is not empty or marked `.uw-subagent-fixture`), and a guard fixture records (read-only `stat`: names, sizes, mtimes) `~/.llmkeys`, `state/` and `service.json` before and after the whole suite and fails on any difference (cr-M10); `USERPROFILE` is pointed at a temp directory for CLI child processes; fake secrets are not needed (no key material is involved), and any fixture that must look like a token is assembled at runtime (memory: `fake-secrets-in-tests-built-at-runtime`).

### 9.1 Offline unit tests (S1, node --test, no CCR)

Harness trick: each router test creates `tmp/spike/uw-router.cjs` (a byte copy of the repo file) and `tmp/state/subagent/` and loads it with `createRequire`, so the real file and its `__dirname`-derived state path are exercised and no ambient env can leak. Also assert the router file has no `process.env` read, no `await` between file open and rename, no `require(` of JSON, and none of V16's banned literals. The seam `route.__test = { reset, now }` (section 4) gives every test an injectable clock and a `globalThis.__uwSub` reset, so TTL, stickiness and throttle tests run without sleeping and in any order. The file under test is `router/uw-router.next.cjs` copied into the temp tree, NEVER required from its repo location (that would resolve to the live `state/subagent`); a contract test fails if a test file requires it directly. A second suite loads the S0b `spike/uw-router.cjs` the same way.

`test/subagent-router.test.mjs`:
- Differential test (REQUIRED, "verify the case it must not break"): load the old router from git (`git show 6edfdee:spike/uw-router.cjs` written to a temp file) and the new one; for 500 generated `asked` values (alias ids, bare ids, `claude-opus-5` variants, empty, unicode, 10 KB strings) with policy ABSENT the outputs are identical EXCEPT the explicit, asserted list of INTENDED DIFFERENCES from git 6edfdee (D-e): (1) a corrupt, empty-model or unreadable `slot.json`: old `undefined` (falls to the anchor), new returns `asked`; (2) the slot trigger is EXACT (`anthropic/claude-opus-5` or bare `claude-opus-5`) where the old one was a substring test, so ids that merely CONTAIN it (`anthropic/claude-opus-5-5`, `claude-opus-5[1m]`, `x-claude-opus-5-y`) now return `asked` instead of the slot's model; (3) decisions and learning use the ORIGINAL `asked`, the rewrite is applied last; (4) after G3 (slot removed) a second differential run asserts that even the exact ids return `asked`. Any other difference fails the test. A test also pins the exact-match rewrite itself (a non-identity `slot.json` rewrites the two exact ids and nothing else).
- Matrix: main vs subagent vs aux by each signal alone and together; agent-id only, billing only, both, neither, each with and without tools; `detectorDisagree` counted; agent-id or billing marker with NO tools is `aux` and returns `asked` unchanged in every mode (cr-M5); a haiku/small-fast asked value under `inherit` returns `asked` (I8).
- Toggle grid: 2 sources x 3 modes (`dynamic`, `inherit`, `free`) x 2 ctx x {main known, main unknown} x {asked in set, tag in set, tag out of set, asked out of set, asked unresolvable}, asserting `act`, `would`, `ret` in shadow and in enforce. `inherit` ignores source; `inherit` + `ctx 1m` + sub-1M main inherits anyway and sets `INHERIT_BELOW_CTX` (D-d); `inherit` with a premium main sets `INHERIT_PREMIUM_MAIN` (D-b); free lists contain only free selectors; ctx lists contain only 1M; the substitute is never below max(asked ctx, 128k) even under `ctx any` (cr-M1); a substitute whose `pb` is below the request's `content-length` or whose `c` is below `1.1 * tokenCount` is skipped and counted; with 12 distinct agentIds the substitutes spread over the top 3 and each agentId always gets the same one (cr-M6); `dropTag` clears `req.builtInSubagentModel` for an out-of-set tag.
- Empty set (D-c): `lists.byProvider[groq]` empty, `lists.all` empty, `empty: true` file: enforce returns `asked` (never main's model, never blocks), status gains `EMPTY_SET` on every occurrence, the decision log gets exactly ONE `empty-set` line per session and reason however many subagents follow, injection text becomes the warning text; a provider listed in `emptyProviders[]` is distinguishable from one absent from `lists.byProvider` for being "not computed".
- Missing / corrupt / oversize (over 1 MiB) / wrong-schema / BOM-prefixed / directory-instead-of-file policy: returns `asked` every time, state recorded, no throw.
- Background-call hazard (both directions, section 6.3): a request with no agent-id and no tools does NOT change main; one with tools but no Agent tool does NOT; one with no session id NEVER teaches (cr-m2); a request WITH an agent-id and no tools never changes main and is never substituted (aux); two sessions interleaved keep separate mains; stale main (past TTL) is ignored; unknown-main in `inherit` returns `asked`.
- Unresolvable candidate: substitute not in `config.Providers` is skipped; none resolvable returns `asked`, counter `unresolvable`.
- Injection: appended once; second call replaces (same bytes); retry on an owned body does not stack; native tag present adds the sentence; size at most 2,048 bytes; only Agent/Task tools touched; OpenAI-shaped `function.description`; `inject: off` and `inherit` leave the body byte-identical (deep-equal before/after).
- Shadow invariant: every decision line has `ret === asked`; the returned value for every input in shadow equals the no-policy return.
- Log redaction and caps: hostile tag text (newlines, quotes, 10 KB, control characters) is logged as `"?"`; no body, header value or config appears in any file written; rotation at 1 MiB leaves at most two files; `sid` truncation and path-traversal session ids (`..\..\x`) never escape the state directory.
- Concurrency: 1,000 interleaved `Promise.all` calls: no torn `main-*.json` (always valid JSON), counters sum to the call count.
- Budget: the 10,000-call timing test of section 6.5 asserts only LOOSE ceilings (p99 about 10 ms; handoff p50 under 3 ms and p99 under 10 ms; D-av), and the STRICT QB-1 numbers are checked by `node test/perf/subagent-router-perf.mjs` on a quiet machine (the verifier runs it).
- Fuzz: random `req` shapes (missing body, tools as non-array, headers null, model numeric): never throws, never returns `undefined` for a non-empty model.
- Stickiness (cr-B2, REQUIRED): one agentId, two turns; between them change main's model AND replace the policy file (new `contentHash`, different set): the returned model is identical both times; an entry older than 6 h (injected clock) is ignored; an unresolvable sticky model is evicted and decided fresh (`stickyEvict`); shadow and `shadow.flag` bypass stickiness and return `asked` on the very next request; every enforced outcome including serve-asked is recorded; an aux request creates no entry; the sticky and `main` maps stay under their caps (256 / 64) after 10,000 distinct ids and expire on the clock seam.
- Exact slot, retirement and classification: `anthropic/claude-opus-5` and bare `claude-opus-5` rewrite per `slot.json`, `claude-opus-5-5` and `claude-opus-5[1m]` do not; corrupt slot returns `asked` and sets `SLOT_ERROR`; policy decisions see the ORIGINAL asked (a non-identity slot does not change what `decide` receives); after G3 the router source contains no `slot.json` string (source scan).
- Reject unresolvable (cr-m6): in enforce an unresolvable `asked` with a resolvable in-set candidate returns the candidate (`reject-unresolvable`); with none returns `asked` and sets `UNRESOLVABLE_ASKED`; one resolvability rule (cr-m7): a fixture grid (disabled provider, `enabled` absent, `models` missing or a string, id with `[1m]`) gives the same verdict in the library and in the router.
- Source scan additions: no `setTimeout`/`setInterval`/`setImmediate` in the router, no top-level `await`, no `require` of anything but `node:fs`/`node:path`/`node:crypto`, no module-scope file access (the file is loaded with every fs function stubbed to throw: loading must not call any); the rendered strings never contain the word "best" (cr-m1) and every row carries a price and a `tools:` marker (D-b, D-f).
- Windows rename (cr-m4): a held-open reader makes the first rename fail with EPERM; the writer retries and succeeds, or skips the flush without throwing; readers retry on EPERM/EBUSY/ENOENT with backoff (simulated by an injected fs layer).
- Helper calls and classification (D-m, REQUIRED): an agent-id request with NO tools returns `asked` unchanged in `dynamic`, `inherit` and `free` and never reaches `decide` (I8a); a tool-carrying haiku or small-fast asked value is judged by the allowed set under `dynamic` and `free` and left alone only under `inherit` (I8b, class `exempt`); `auxOnRelay` and the aux-by-model-and-bytes counters move on aux requests and are bounded (16 models); the classification log gets exactly one line per request with the closed field set of section 5.5 and NO body, header value or message text (a hostile system prompt and tool description never appear in any file written); the sampled fields `sysb` and `bb` fall in their buckets at the boundaries; a billing-only subagent (no agent id) shares one sticky key and one hash per session and asked model and increments `blNoAgentId`.
- Free promise break and unknown values (M3): in `free` mode an empty set, an unknown main, no usable substitute or an unresolvable asked each serve `asked`, add `FREE_PROMISE_BREAK`, count `freeBreak` and log once per session and reason; unknown context (`c = 0`) fails a substitute and passes the honour path with `CTX_UNKNOWN`; unknown payload cap passes in both paths and is counted `payloadUnknown`.

`test/subagent-policy.test.mjs` (library, snapshot and bench FIXTURES, not the real files): chat-capable rule (`chat?` admitted, pool/ocr/image excluded), selector spelling against a fake `Providers` (relay bare spelling, `[1m]` collapse, unresolvable dropped), free rule (null price not free, FREE vs FREE?), 1M rule (`ctx null` + `[1m]`, `ctx 999999` fails), bench ok versus pay/auth/rate (counted not pruned), observed overlay beating a probe, funnel arithmetic, rank order and TTFT buckets and byte-stable output, per-provider diversity cap, inject text size cap and ASCII, `contentHash` stable across recompiles, empty-set detection per provider, fragile-set warning, stale snapshot warns, missing snapshot refuses, gateway-down fallback stamps `providersLive:false`. Counts in assertions carry their fixture denominator. Added in revision 2: `emptyProviders[]` correctness and the distinction from "not computed"; premium flag by family and by price threshold; `pb`, `payloadRisk` and `payloadUnknown` counts; `ctxHints` for the six aliases; id sanitiser (a 65-character id and an id with a space or a control character are rejected and counted `id-rejected`, never reach `inject.*`); unverified counts in every output (D-f); `inherit` computes no lists and warns on a premium or sub-1M main; the substitute top-K selection is deterministic across recompiles; `--mode fixed` is rejected; the compiler reads `state/tool-fidelity.json` directly and a missing file means all `u`; hashing excludes timestamps. Revision 3 additions, FREE-SCOPE FIXTURES (D-j, D-k; fixtures only, never the real registry): a registry fixture with provider rows of every tier (`free`, `free-deposit`, `paid`, `subscription`, `management`) and a snapshot fixture whose models vary in free tag (`x:free`, `x-free`, `a/free/b`, `x_free`, `x.free`, `free-x`, `free_x`, and the non-tags `freedom`, `freeform`, `free2`, `carefree`), listed price 0 or not, badge `FREE`/`FREE?`/none, and bench status `ok`/`pay`/`gone`/`auth`. Assertions: (1) `free models` admits badge or tag rows, flags `PRICED_BUT_BADGED`, never admits `subscription` or `management` rows; (2) `free providers` admits EVERY probe-ok model on a `free`-tier provider and nothing from other tiers; (3) `free providers + deposit` equals `free providers` plus ONLY tag-matching probe-ok models on `free-deposit` and `paid` providers, and the PAID-PROVIDER RULE: an untagged probe-ok model on a paid or deposit provider is SKIPPED even when its price is 0 and even when its badge is `FREE` or `FREE?` (counted `deposit-strict-skipped`), a tagged model with status `pay` or `gone` is excluded, and no per-provider override exists; (4) `FREE_TAG` positives and negatives exactly as listed; (5) the `subscription` relay row is in no scope under any mode; (6) a row whose key id is 47 characters long, with a snapshot `keyId` clipped to 30, is tiered CORRECTLY from the registry (the compiler gives the same result when the snapshot keyId field is deleted or garbled: it never reads it); (7) a provider with two keys uses the tier of the key chosen by `key-choices.json` (and a missing choice for a multi-key provider is the existing loud error, not a guess); (8) unreadable or absent registry makes `providers` and `providers+deposit` refuse with `TIER_UNREADABLE` while `models` still compiles; (9) rank key 2b: inside `free providers` price-0 rows precede priced rows, priced rows ascend, unknown price is last, and the same rows under `dynamic` are NOT reordered by price; (10) per-scope funnel counts are present and equal the independently computed fixture counts for all three scopes regardless of the chosen scope; (11) a model flipped to `pay` in the observed-overlay fixture leaves the compiled lists only after `rebuild`, and `rebuild --if-stale yes` is a no-op when nothing changed; (12) the tool-fidelity tiers: relay rows compile as `v` with basis `provenance`, a probed `t` row prints "tools work at small payload", and a `tools: false` row with an L1+L2 pass is eligible as `t` (the claim is ignored once probed). Revision 4 additions: (13) FREE_TAG is tested on the BARE id: a fixture row `x:free[1m]` (and its bare sibling `x:free`) is admitted by `free models` and by the tag rule on a paid provider, while the raw pattern alone would miss it; (14) `free models` on a `paid` or `free-deposit` provider skips a badge-only row and a price-0 untagged row (counted `deposit-strict-skipped`) and admits a tagged one (M5), and an unknown tier applies the strict rule; (15) pool aliases (D-n): rows matching `POOL_ALIAS_RE` are flagged `al`, counted separately, capped at tier `u` even with an L1+L2 pass recorded `alias:true`, ranked below an otherwise equal non-alias row, and `kilo-auto/free` is an alias that also carries the tag; (16) `substitutable` counts, `emptyProviders[]` and `thinProviders[]` are computed from the substitute pool: a provider whose whole set has unknown context is EMPTY for refusal purposes while its set is non-empty; (17) rank key 2b order (REVISED in revision 8 item 2) is class 0 (price 0 on a free-tier provider), then class 0t (free-tag-admitted rows on paid or deposit providers), then unknown price, then positive ascending, with `price = pin + pout` (sum, or the listed side alone), only under `mode free` with scope `providers` or `providers+deposit`, and the same rows under `dynamic` and `models` are not reordered; plus the four tests of revision 8 item 2 (class 0 above 0t whatever the TTFT or context, top-3 spread on the new order, acceptance 4-7 counts unchanged, free-tier-only ranking byte-identical); (18) `fnv1a32` known vectors (empty string, `a`, `foobar`) and the top-3 spread over 12 agent ids; (19) `TIER_MISMATCH` when a fixture snapshot `keyId` tier differs from the registry tier; `E_TIER_UNREADABLE` for a non-existent or corrupt registry and for a PRESENT but corrupt `key-choices.json` (an absent one means no choices); `E_KEY_AMBIGUOUS` when `chooseKeys` throws for a multi-key provider with no choice; (20) the picker label output of `menu/style.mjs` is byte-identical before and after it imports `POOL_ALIAS_RE`, and the alternatives of `keysync/keysync.mjs` `POOL_IDS` are a subset of it. Revision 8 additions: (21) G17, BUILT: an `excluded`-tier provider (fixture `fx-mgmt`) contributes nothing to any mode, source, ctx or scope, to `models[]`, lists, `substitutable`, ranking, any `counts.*` figure, `chosenScopeN`, the injected text or the hash, and `explain` prints the excluded-tier line; (22) `noRowProviders` (S4b, revision 8 item 1): the derived fixture values, disjointness from `emptyProviders[]`, `[]` under `inherit`, the hash covers it, `__proto__`/`constructor` provider names are listed without touching a prototype, `fx-mgmt` in neither list.

`test/subagent-cli.test.mjs`: spawns `node keysync/key.mjs subagent-policy ...` with `--policy-file` and `--state-dir` pointing at temp files and a fixture snapshot via a documented test-only flag set; asserts: a REAL first `set` without `--source`/`--mode` fails with usage (a DRY first `set` without `--source` previews as `all-providers` with a NOTE, revision 7 item 1); `--dry yes` writes nothing; empty-set refusal exit code 2 with the stage name; `--allow-empty yes` writes and warns; a bare `--dry` followed by a word is rejected (V14 swallow guard); corrupt owner file is a loud error on `show` and `set`; ambient env var is ignored with a printed line; `clear` removes all three kinds of file; `explain` exit 3 for an unknown id; `show` with no files prints "policy off". Revision 2 additions: `--mode fixed` fails with "renamed to inherit"; refusal only when empty for every provider or for main's current provider, otherwise exit 0 with the `emptyProviders[]` warning and a saved file (D-c); `--allow-empty yes` overrides; `rollback` writes `shadow.flag` and flips the owner file with NO snapshot, bench or providers input present (run with all inputs missing: still exit 0), and `set` clears it; any test-only `--*-file` flag without the full set of state flags is refused [REVISED, rule as built in revision 9 item 2: a required flag set per subcommand, `rollback`/`clear` accept `--state-dir` alone and refuse `--policy-file` alone, blank values rejected, protected folders refused, an optional flag not given resolves under `<state>/_not-given/<flag>`, `clear` on an explicit path needs it to parse as an owner file]; every run is checked by the no-touch guard (section 9.1 principle). Free-scope additions: `--free-scope` is accepted only with `--mode free` (a call with another mode is refused with a message), defaults to `providers`, and prints the three scope funnels under the names `free models`, `free providers`, `free providers + deposit` (D-l); `--registry-file` and `--key-choices-file` follow the fixture-mode rule of the other test-only flags (revision 7 item 7; for `retier`, `--registry-file` and `--choices-file` go together); an exit-2 refusal on an empty scope names the scope. Revision 4 additions: the BOOLEAN VOCABULARY (`--dry no` is the same as absent; `--dry`, `--dry foo`, `--dry true` and `--dry --source x` each exit 1 with the accepted values listed); the complete exit-code table of section 7 (each code has at least one asserting case: usage 1, empty refusal 2, unknown model 3, corrupt owner file 4, unreadable registry 4, corrupt key choices 4, ambiguous key 4, simulated write failure 5); `--allow provider/model` and `--tool-fidelity-file` (explain reads only the fixture file); every acceptance command of section 12 S1 is executed verbatim through `test/fixtures/subagent-flags.mjs`; `show` and `set` print the plain helper-call statement of D-m.

`test/keysync-tiers.test.mjs` (S1c, fixtures and a fake vault only): (1) the vocabulary has exactly the five tiers and `management` is the only `excluded` one; a contract test reads the literal `r.tier !== "management"` out of `keysync/keysync.mjs` and `refresh/cli.mjs` and asserts it equals the vocabulary `excluded` set (so a sixth excluded tier cannot be added in one place only); (2) `key.mjs add` with a typo (`freee`, `Paid`, `deposit`, empty) exits 1, prints the list of valid tiers, writes nothing and never reaches the PowerShell step (the runner is a spy that must not be called); a valid tier including `free-deposit` reaches it; (3) the env var rule: for every row of a 78-row registry fixture the derived `envVarName` equals the stored one, and `free-deposit` derives `...FREE_DEPOSIT`; (4) `retier` over the INJECTED FAKE VAULT (in-memory `{get, set, remove}`; the real Credential Manager is never touched and the test fails if the production vault module is imported): the secret is readable under the new id, absent under the old id, byte-identical, registry row changed in `id`, `tier` and `envVarName` only (`added`, `bucket`, `provider`, `notes` unchanged), `key-choices.json` updated only when it named the old id; (5) crash injection after each step (after write-new, after registry update, after choices update, before remove): both secrets are never lost, a re-run completes the move, and the old secret is removed last; (6) refusals: unknown id, tier not in the vocabulary, same tier, new id already present, `--dry yes` touching nothing; (7) the secret value never appears in stdout, stderr, any log line, the registry or the choices file (the fake secret is built at runtime from split pieces, memory `fake-secrets-in-tests-built-at-runtime`, and searched for in every captured output). Revision 4 changes to this file (M9): the seam is `{retierCopy, deleteCredential, exists}` (section 7.1) and crash injection happens at its boundaries (after `retierCopy` before key choices, after key choices before delete); the RESUME branch is tested in every crash state (both credentials present with the old registry row; new registry row with a key-choices file still naming the OLD id, which must make `chooseKeys` raise the ambiguity error for a multi-key provider fixture until the resume, and must be harmless for a single-key one; new row with the old credential still present; fully finished, which exits 0 with "nothing to do"); the registry fixture is written ONLY by the fake vault (node never rewrites it); the static test asserts the production script text contains the ids but no secret variable on any command line.

`test/subagent-contract.test.mjs`: (1) router header/tag/tool-name literals equal `menu/cc-contract.mjs` and `menu/ccr-client.mjs` constants; (2) no new file outside the two contract modules matches V16's needles (the existing scan already covers `spike/*.cjs`); (3) R1 guard: the policy library, the CLI branch and the router contain no `saveConfig`, no `rpc("saveConfig"`, no `spawn`/`exec` of `run.mjs`, no write method other than `getConfig`; the doctor check is read-only; (4) `restartRelevantFingerprint` of a config before and after an arbitrary `CUSTOM_ROUTER_PATH` or policy-file change is identical (pins R1 against future drift of V5); (5) source-order pin in the style of V17 that the router returns before any await-bearing path and that `finally` budget code cannot throw. Revision 2 additions: (6) no test file requires `router/uw-router.next.cjs` from its repo location; (7) `harness/deploy-router.mjs` runs `node --check`, the exact-bytes smoke test, backup and write-temp-plus-rename in that order (source-order pin) and never writes the live path directly; (8) the router, library and CLI contain none of the string `fixed` as a mode value; (9) `Router.fallback.mode` is asserted `off` by the doctor check. Revision 4 additions: (10) the directory list of the tag and data-folder scan includes `router` (`test/contracts.test.mjs:282`, M-b) and passes on `router/uw-router.next.cjs` and `router/uw-router.s0b.cjs`; (11) `menu/ccr-client.mjs` changes are additive only (a fixture pins `routableFromConfig` and the existing exports before and after, M7); (12) `menu/style.mjs` and the compiler import the SAME `POOL_ALIAS_RE` from `menu/pool-rule.mjs` and `keysync/keysync.mjs` `POOL_IDS` stays a subset (D-n, no third fork); (14, revision 5) `menu/subagent-funnel.mjs` and `menu/tiers.mjs` import nothing outside `menu/pool-rule.mjs` and `menu/tiers.mjs`, name no `node:` module and no `refresh/` or `keysync/` path (so the picker may import them, `test/bench-counts.test.mjs:253`), and `keysync/subagent-policy.mjs` imports `funnel` from `menu/subagent-funnel.mjs` (the single-source rule behind D-o); (13) the deploy script's smoke test AWAITS the async route function (a source check plus a candidate whose async route rejects must be refused, M-f).

### 9.2 Sandbox end-to-end (S0 experiments and the S3 gate): 3.0.22 isolated harness, or unit level?

Decision: both, with a strict split. Unit tests cover the algorithm; the isolated 3.0.22 harness (V15) is used ONLY for what needs a real CCR: E4 body edit reaches upstream, E5 added header reaches upstream and logs, E7 `globalThis` persists across the per-request require-cache deletion, E6 resolution behaviour of selectors, and the final "real CCR + real router file + synthetic subagent-shaped request is served by the substitute" proof. Everything else stays out of the sandbox because sandboxes cost risk (three real incidents, memory `ccr-takeover-breaks-sandbox-isolation`). Revision 2 additions to that list: A0 (the enricher gate is satisfied, V22/V25), E12 (`content-length` and `tokenCount` reach the router), E13 (clearing `req.builtInSubagentModel` works), A12 (stickiness and the thinking-block pass-through) and the deploy-protocol rehearsal of `harness/deploy-router.mjs` against a scratch tree (a broken candidate must be refused, a restore must work).

Design (`harness/stub-upstream.mjs`, `harness/subagent-e2e.mjs`, port constant `STUB_PORT = 39459` added beside `harness/config.mjs:29-33`):
- No real key, no network egress: the only provider is `uwstub` with `type: "anthropic_messages"`, `api_base_url: "http://127.0.0.1:39459"`, `api_key: "stub"`, models `["m-main","m-free","m-big"]`, `autoFetchModels:false`, saved with `applyProfile:false` exactly like `harness/probe-anthropic.mjs:21-34` and `harness/spike.mjs:68-100`, under `assertPayloadIsolated(cfg, {allowProviders:true})`, `assertIsolatedConfig`, `assertRouterClean`, and `assertDesktopSyncLandedInScratch` after the save.
- The stub records, per request, ONLY: the request `model`, the sha256 and byte length of the body, the Agent tool `description` (our own injected text; no user data exists in synthetic bodies), the presence and value of `anthropic-beta` and `x-uw-subpolicy`, then answers a minimal valid Anthropic SSE stream.
- The router under test is a COPY at `harness/scratch/spike/uw-router.cjs` with state at `harness/scratch/state/subagent/` (location-derived, section 4); the live router file is never referenced. A copy-equals-source hash assertion runs first (source = `router/uw-router.next.cjs`, or the throwaway S0 probe router). HOW `CUSTOM_ROUTER_PATH` IS SET (cr-M2: no harness script sets it today, V25, and `assertRouterClean` neither requires nor forbids it): `harness/subagent-e2e.mjs` sets `CUSTOM_ROUTER_PATH` to the absolute path of the scratch copy in the SANDBOX config only, through the sandbox's own `saveConfig` on the sandbox core port (39457), after `assertIsolatedConfig` and `assertPayloadIsolated(cfg, {allowProviders:true})` pass and with `Router.fallback.mode` kept `off`; it then reads the config back and asserts the value is the scratch path and contains no `\.uw\spike\` substring. The live `CUSTOM_ROUTER_PATH` is never read or written.
- Isolation protocol, written into the script and each item asserted, not eyeballed (memories `sandbox-port-isolation-gotcha`, `sandbox-isolation-verify-full-surface`): (1) before anything: record sha256 of `~/.claude/settings.json`, the exact live value of `env.ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL`, listener PIDs on 3456, 3457, 3458, the HKCU user PATH, the `~/Downloads` listing (all of this is `makeTripwire()` already, `harness/guard.mjs:82-105`, plus the listener-PID baseline); (2) start with `harness/start.ps1`, which forces `CCR_INTERNAL_APP_DATA_DIR`, `CCR_CONFIG_DIR`, `LOCALAPPDATA`, `CCR_WEB_HOST/PORT/AUTH_TOKEN` in the command itself; (3) FORCE every port field on this rebuild: `gateway.host/port/corePort`, `HOST`, `PORT` (the bootstrap does, `harness/bootstrap-live-safe.mjs:38-40`) and VERIFY by `listenerPid()` that 39456, 39457, 39458, 39459 are held by the sandbox PID and that 3456-3458 are still held by the SAME live PIDs recorded in (1); (4) immediately after daemon start and after each `saveConfig`, run `tripwire.assert()` and additionally assert live settings `env.ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL` is byte-identical (the memory's "expect no match" check does not apply now, because live settings legitimately route through CCR); (5) teardown scrubs `Providers[]` (never disables the profile subsystem, `harness/guard.mjs:196-223`), stops the stub, asserts 39456-39459 closed and the live PIDs unchanged.
- Use `harness/bootstrap-live-safe.mjs` (live gateway stays up; its header explains why) not `bootstrap.mjs`.
- Requests are synthetic and sent with `fetch` to `http://127.0.0.1:39456/v1/messages` (pattern `harness/spike.mjs:129-136`, EXTENDED, because that pattern sends only `x-api-key` and no `User-Agent`, V25, and CCR's enricher would not run, V22). Every request carries: (1) the API key of the sandbox's own ENABLED claude-code PROFILE (the script creates that profile in the scratch config, `profile.enabled !== false`, `agent: "claude-code"`, and sends ITS key, because the enricher matches the request's `x-auth-api-key-id`, which CCR derives from the key used), not the bare gateway key; (2) `User-Agent: claude-cli/2.1.0 (external, cli)` (any value whose lower-case contains `claude`); (3) `routing.enhancedRoute` left at its default (not `false`). Shapes: main-shaped (Agent tool present, `x-claude-code-session-id`, no agent-id), subagent-shaped (`x-claude-code-agent-id`, the billing system block `x-anthropic-billing-header: cc_is_subagent=true`, at least one tool, optional tag line), aux-shaped (agent-id, no tools), background-shaped (no agent-id, no tools). PRE-ASSERTION A0 (runs before every other assertion and aborts the script on failure): a probe subagent-shaped request carrying a tag reaches the scratch router with `req.builtInClaudeCodeSubagent === true` and `req.builtInSubagentModel` set (the S0 probe router records both into the stub-visible log); if not, the enricher gate is not satisfied and every later PASS would be meaningless.
- S0 orchestrator gates (revision 9 item 4; exact flag text as built in Revision 9, see `node harness/subagent-e2e.mjs --plan`): `--plan`, `--teardown`, the owner act `--approve-plan` together with `--g1-approved`, refusal on a dirty scratch tree, a tree-wide isolation proof (daemon plus every descendant) and retained redacted evidence in `harness\g1-evidence\<timestamp>\`. The probe router is the tracked `harness/probe-router.cjs`; the router under test is still the scratch COPY described above.
- Assertions the script prints as PASS/FAIL lines: A0 the enricher pre-assertion above; A1 policy file absent: stub receives exactly the requested model; A2 shadow with a tag naming `uwstub/m-free`: stub receives `asked`, decision log has `would: uwstub/m-free`, `ret: asked`; A3 enforce + dynamic + tag in set: stub receives `m-free`; A4 enforce + tag NOT in set: stub receives the deterministic substitute (and `req.builtInSubagentModel` was cleared, E13); A5 enforce + inherit: stub receives main's model learned from the main-shaped request, and an aux-shaped request and a haiku-asked request are served as asked; A6 injection: the stub's captured Agent description contains the marker once, byte length at most 2,048 more than baseline, and a SECOND identical request does not stack; A7 added header present at the stub (E5) or recorded as ABSENT (a finding, not a failure); A8 `globalThis` counter increments across requests (E7); A9 empty set returns requested model, `EMPTY_SET` appears in `status.json` and exactly one `empty-set` line is logged for the session; A10 a policy listing an unresolvable selector is skipped, and the trace shows no `custom-model-not-configured` for the returned string; A11 the live gateway PID and `service.json` are untouched; A12 stickiness (cr-B2): a subagent's second request after main and policy changed is served by the same model, and after `shadow.flag` appears by `asked`; the same test sends a body whose assistant history contains a `thinking` block with a `signature` across the model switch caused by the flag and asserts the stub received that body byte-identical (the router never edits messages); whether a real upstream returns a 400 for a signature from another Anthropic model is E16, not answerable against a stub; A13 a request whose `content-length` or `tokenCount` exceeds a candidate's `pb` or `c` skips that candidate (E12).
- This run starts a SECOND CCR 3.0.22 daemon while the live one runs (precedent: `bootstrap-live-safe`). It needs owner gate G1 (section 12) before the first execution, and is re-run after any CCR or Claude Code update.

### 9.2b Fixture specification (S1.0; every S1 acceptance count comes from these rows)

Files under `test/fixtures/subagent/`, materialised into a temp directory by `node test/fixtures/subagent-flags.mjs --dir D` (which prints the flag set of section 12 S1): `snapshot.json` (schema 9 shape: `rows[]` with `keyId`, `provider`, `models[]` of `{id, ctx, pin, pout, badge, tools, outModality: "chat", mode: false, provenance}`), `bench.json` (`{schema, generatedAt, models: {"provider/id": {s, t, a}}}`, `s` the bench status), `providers.json` (`{Providers: [{name, models, enabled}]}` for `--providers-file`), `registry.json` (the 7-field row shape of V28), `vault-providers.json` (the `providers.json` list `filterRegistry` needs: one row per provider with `protocol` not `generic`), `key-choices.json` (`{}`), `observed.json` (an empty overlay in the format `loadBench` reads), `default-model.json` (`anthropic/claude-sonnet-5-5[1m]`), `settings.json` (an `env` with the six alias values, haiku and small-fast among them, and no `CLAUDE_CODE_SUBAGENT_MODEL`), and an absent `tool-fidelity.json` (every row `u`; S5 tests add a populated one). The rows (`-` = unknown; prices are `pin/pout`):

| Provider (registry tier) | Models (bench status; ctx; price; badge) | Purpose |
|---|---|---|
| `anthropic` (relay row, key id `relay.anthropic.subscription`, no registry row: tier `subscription`) | `claude-sonnet-5-5` (ok; 1M; 3/15), `claude-haiku-4-5` (ok; 200k; 1/5), `claude-opus-5` (ok; 1M; 15/75) | main provider; in NO free scope; haiku is the `exempt` value |
| `fx-free-a` (`free`) | `fxa-alpha` (ok; 200k; 0/0; FREE?), `fxa-beta` (ok; 128k; 0.1/0.4; PAID), `fxa-gamma` (ok; -; -; none), `fxa-big:free[1m]` (ok; 1M; 0/0; FREE?) AND its bare sibling `fxa-big:free` (ok), `auto` (ok; -; -; none; ALIAS), `kilo-auto/free` (ok; -; -; none; ALIAS and tag), `fxa-dead` (gone) | all free-provider rules, rank key 2b, the `[1m]` collapse and bare-id tag test (M2), aliases |
| `fx-free-b` (`free`) | `fxb-one` (ok; -; -; none) | substitute pool EMPTY while the set is non-empty (M3) |
| `fx-dep` (`free-deposit`) | `fxd-model:free` (ok; 131072; -), `fxd-plain` (ok; 128k; 0/0; FREE?), `fxd-old-free` (pay) | strict rule: tagged admitted, untagged price-0 skipped, tagged-but-`pay` excluded |
| `fx-paid` (`paid`) | `fxp-plain` (ok; 128k; -; FREE), `fxp/free/x` (ok; 128k; -) | strict rule on `paid`, tag in a path segment |
| `fx-mgmt` (`management`) | `fxm-one` (ok; -; -; FREE) | excluded by `filterRegistry` and, G17, contributes to no count, list, rank or hash in any scope |

Counts the S1 acceptance asserts (derived from the rows): probe-ok selectors after the `[1m]` collapse and without the relay: `fx-free-a` 6, `fx-free-b` 1, `fx-dep` 2, `fx-paid` 2. `free models` = 5 (`fxa-alpha`, `fxa-big:free`, `kilo-auto/free`, `fxd-model:free`, `fxp/free/x`), of which 1 with ctx >= 1M (`fxa-big:free`). `free providers` = 7 on 2 providers. `free providers + deposit` = 9 on 4 providers. `deposit-strict-skipped` = 2 (`fxd-plain`, `fxp-plain`). ALIAS rows = 2. Under `dynamic` with `all-providers` (G17, revision 8 item 3): 14 allowed rows out of 14 in the chosen scope (the relay 3, `fx-free-a` 6, `fx-free-b` 1, `fx-dep` 2, `fx-paid` 2; `fxm-one` is NOT counted: `fx-mgmt` is an `excluded` tier), of which verified 3 (the relay, by provenance), small 0, unverified 11, and 10 substitutable (relay 3, `fx-free-a` 3, `fx-free-b` 0, `fx-dep` 2, `fx-paid` 2); the population was 15 before G17. `substitutable`: `fx-free-a` 3, `fx-free-b` 0, `fx-dep` 1, `fx-paid` 1, so `emptyProviders[]` = [`fx-free-b`] and `thinProviders[]` (1 or 2) = [`fx-dep`, `fx-paid`] under `providers+deposit`. Rank under `free providers`: price class 0 (`fxa-alpha`, `fxa-big:free`) before unknown (`fxa-gamma`, `fxb-one`, then the two aliases last by key 5) before positive (`fxa-beta`). Under `free providers + deposit` the two free-tag rows on paid or deposit providers (`fxd-model:free`, `fxp/free/x`) rank as class 0t (revision 8 item 2): below `fxa-alpha` and `fxa-big:free` (class 0), above the unknown-price rows; the `free providers` ranking is the same before and after that fix. `noRowProviders` derived from these rows (S4b): `free providers` [`anthropic`, `fx-dep`, `fx-paid`], `free providers + deposit` [`anthropic`], `free models` [`anthropic`, `fx-free-b`], `dynamic` all-providers []. The relay has no free-scope row, so `same-provider` with the fixture main is empty under every scope (exit 2).

### 9.3 Observability checks (S2 onward)

`status.json` counters and `decisions.jsonl` are the ground truth for shadow; CCR's `request_route_traces` hop `customer.custom-router` and `request_logs.requested_model` / `resolved_model` give the independent cross-check that `ret` is what CCR actually routed; a read-only `subagent-policy report` (S4) joins them by session prefix and a +/- 2 s window.

### 9.4 Toggle pane tests (S4b, D-o; `test/subagent-pane.test.mjs` plus the updated legend test; fixtures of 9.2b only, never the real `~/.llmkeys`, `state/` or snapshot)

1. Rendering: the pane frame at terminal widths 80, 100 and 132, in ASCII (a `TERM=dumb`-style caps object) and in Unicode mode, with colour on and off: every line is exactly the frame width (`g.width`), no line is longer, the content of the 10.1 sketch is present, and below `FRAME_MIN` the existing too-narrow notice is shown instead.
2. PARITY with the CLI, the central test: for EVERY raw combination of 2 sources x 3 modes x 3 free scopes x 2 ctx (36; 20 distinct) the pane figure for models included, the three free-scope counts, the tier split, ALIAS, not-substitutable, `emptyProviders[]`, `noRowProviders` (revision 8 item 1) and `thinProviders[]` equals what `node keysync/key.mjs subagent-policy set --dry yes` prints for the same flags on the same fixtures (parsed from its report) and the `counts` of the compiled file it would write; the test also asserts that the pane and the CLI shell import the SAME module path for `funnel`.
3. Live update on keypress: a reducer-level test opens the pane with `ctrl+t`, presses `m`, `f`, `c`, `s`, left/right and space, and asserts the counts after each key against a table; `f` is inert unless the mode is `free`; a spy on `funnel` shows ONE call per toggle change and ZERO calls when the same state is rendered 100 times (no per-frame recompute); the memo makes a repeated tuple free.
4. Empty-set warning: with the fixture main on the relay provider and `free providers` under `same provider` the pane shows EMPTY and the `FREE PROMISE` line, and `enter` is refused with the `E_EMPTY` text and writes nothing. Revision 8 additions: with the fixture relay as main under `free providers` and `all providers` the pane shows the `NO ROWS` row naming `anthropic` (it is in `noRowProviders`, not in `emptyProviders[]`), `enter` is NOT refused (rule (b) applies only under `same provider`), and the picker note appears when that provider is picked as main.
5. No write until Enter: any key sequence that ends in `esc` leaves a fingerprint (names, sizes, mtimes) of the fixture `llmkeys` and `state` directories unchanged; `enter` on a valid combination writes exactly the owner file, byte-for-byte equal to the one the CLI `set` writes for the same toggles, preserves `enforcement`, `inject`, `unverified`, `classLog` and `allow`, writes NO compiled file and starts NO process (the pane module and its imports contain no `child_process`, a source scan; the post-exit rebuild belongs to the wrapper, not the pane, items 12-15).
6. Startup budget and import graph unchanged: `test/bench-startup.mjs` still reports a first frame within 300 ms; `test/bench-counts.test.mjs:253` still passes; a new assertion pins that the static import graph of `menu/uwpick.mjs` contains NONE of `menu/subagent-funnel.mjs`, `menu/subagent-pane.mjs`, `menu/subagent-state.mjs`, `menu/tiers.mjs`; and that `menu/subagent-funnel.mjs` imports only `menu/pool-rule.mjs` and `menu/tiers.mjs` and no `node:` module.
7. Cost: median recompute under 20 ms and the open pre-pass under 50 ms on a synthetic 6,432-row snapshot fixture (the real row count), numbers printed, CI limits 5x to absorb noise.
8. Legend and key map: the `KEYS` list of `menu/legend.mjs` contains the `ctrl+t` row and the legend renders the pane entry AND the permanent AUTO-APPLY warning (D-p, item 14) at 80/100/132; a table test drives the reducer with every control byte and asserts `ctrl+t` is bound only to the pane and no other binding changed; the existing legend test that enumerates keys is updated.
9. Staleness and sources: touching the fixture observed file after the pane loaded shows `STALE`, and `r` clears it and recomputes; the "counts as of" row carries the snapshot, bench and compiled stamps; an absent compiled file shows `n/a: run subagent-policy rebuild (tiers unknown)` for the two provider scopes and the strict fallback for `free models`; a compiled file whose `counts.allowed` differs from the pane figure for the same tuple adds the `compiled says N` line.
10. Main provider: several `main-<sid>.json` fixtures: the newest inside the 6 h TTL is used, several providers show `newest of N` and `p` cycles them, none gives `main: unknown` with the per-provider list, a file older than the TTL is ignored, and a hostile file name never makes the reader leave the state directory.
11. `docs/qa-interactive-protocol.md` gains the steps (open the pane, change each toggle and watch the counts, `esc` leaves no file, `enter` writes the owner file, the legend lists `ctrl+t`, a stale-overlay case, the AUTO-APPLY warning visible on every open, quit after a save and see one rebuild, quit with no save and see none, a forced rebuild failure shown on the next open), run by hand with the existing protocol.
12. AUTO-REBUILD, UNCHANGED SETTINGS (D-p condition b): `test/uwpick-autorebuild.test.mjs` drives the wrapper's post-exit step (the S4b executor chooses the seam, for example a `-PostExitOnly -BeforeHash <h>` switch on `menu/uwpick-run.ps1` plus fixture variables for the owner file and the rebuild command; the seam may exist only for tests and must not change the default launch path) with a COUNTING STUB in place of `node keysync/key.mjs`. Cases: owner file byte-identical before and after (including a save that rewrote the same bytes, and a change followed by a revert), and absent before and after: the stub is called ZERO times and no process is spawned.
13. AUTO-REBUILD, CHANGED SETTINGS: a different hash (first save with no owner file before, a changed toggle, an owner-file edit made by the CLI during the picker session) runs the rebuild EXACTLY ONCE, with exactly the arguments `subagent-policy rebuild --if-stale yes --quiet yes`, bounded by the timeout (a stub that hangs is killed at the timeout), and the stub never sees any enforcement flag (`--enforce`, `--inject`) or any `ccr`/gateway command; the owner file's `enforcement` and `inject` values are identical before and after the step.
14. WARNING TEXT: the pane render and the legend render both contain the required phrase `closing the picker after a saved change automatically runs` followed by `subagent-policy rebuild --if-stale yes --quiet yes` (the command may wrap across two lines but is never cut), at widths 80, 100 and 132, ASCII and Unicode, colour on and off; it is present with a clean state, with unsaved changes, after a save and after a refused Enter, and NO key (the reducer table test of item 8 sends every control byte and every pane key) removes it.
15. FAILURE IS NON-FATAL AND SURFACES: a stub that exits non-zero, a stub that cannot be started and a timeout each leave the wrapper exit code and the picker's `/model` handoff output unchanged, write `state/subagent/autorebuild.json` with `ok: false`, and the NEXT picker open shows it (banner and pane line `auto-rebuild failed (exit N, time): run subagent-policy rebuild`) while the owner-file-newer-than-compiled `policy not applied` state stays visible; a later successful run clears it; the doctor fixture with a failed record WARNs `AUTOREBUILD_FAILED`.

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

## 10. Surfaces: doctor, picker banner, visibility

Doctor check `subagent-policy` (pure `checkSubagentPolicy({owner, compiled, status, cfgSummary, settingsEnv, slot, tiersNow, overlay, accuracy, now})` (revision 4, M-c: `tiersNow` is the provider-to-tier map read from the registry at doctor time, compared with `compiled.builtFrom.tiersHash`; `overlay` is the parsed observed overlay, compared with the compiled free lists for `FREE_LIST_STALE`; `accuracy` is the recorded classifier-accuracy result of section 12.5; all three are passed in, never read inside the pure function) in `menu/doctor.mjs`, read-only; unit-tested like `checkCcrPatch`; `cfgSummary` comes from the same read-only `getConfig` the doctor already uses): FAIL when `CUSTOM_ROUTER_PATH` is unset, OR resolves (after `path.resolve` and case-folding) to a file other than the repo's `spike/uw-router.cjs`, OR that file is missing (D-e: the policy is inert otherwise, and today nothing asserts it, V3, V26), when the compiled file is corrupt or older than the owner file, when `Router.fallback.mode` is `retry` or `model-chain` (a chained model would mask a failing route), or when `env.CLAUDE_CODE_SUBAGENT_MODEL` is set or `availableModels`/`deniedModels` exist; WARN (standing, every doctor run) `N unverified models allowed` while `compiled.counts.unverified > 0` under `unverified: allow-warn` (D-f; also a `status.policy.unverifiedAllowed` field, cr-m9), and WARN on `empty: true`, on any `emptyProviders[]` entry that is main's current provider, on `NO_ROWS_FOR_MAIN` (S4b, revision 8 item 1: main's current provider is in `noRowProviders`, that is it has ZERO rows under the current toggles, for example the Anthropic relay under mode `free`), on any `status.warnings` (including `INHERIT_PREMIUM_MAIN`, `INHERIT_BELOW_CTX`, `PAYLOAD_RISK`, `UNRESOLVABLE_ASKED`), on a compiled free scope (`free providers` or `free providers + deposit`) whose provider tiers can no longer be read from the vault registry or whose `tiersHash` differs from the registry now (D-k: a `retier` or `add` happened, run `rebuild`), on `FREE_LIST_STALE` (the live overlay shows models in the compiled free lists now `pay` or `auth`: they are named, and `rebuild` clears it, D-j), on `FREE_PROMISE_BREAK` (mode free served a model outside the free set, section 6.2 note h), on `CLASSIFIER_UNMEASURED` (enforcement is on but no passing classifier-accuracy record exists or it is older than the current Claude Code and CCR versions, section 12.5), on `TIER_MISMATCH`, on `blNoAgentId` greater than 0 (a subagent without an agent id: E1 expects none), on a free scope in use while a provider listed in it is labelled `free` in the registry but its bench record shows `pay` for most of its models (a probable mislabel), on `AUTOREBUILD_FAILED` (the last picker-exit auto-rebuild failed, read from `state/subagent/autorebuild.json`, passed in with `status`; D-p), on `shadow.flag` present ("router is acting as shadow"), on `NATIVE_MENU_PRESENT` (a provider has `modelDescriptions`), on snapshot older than 7 days, on `providersHash` mismatch with the live `Providers`, on `slot.json` not the identity (until G3 removes the slot), on a router file that is not the policy-aware version, on a `spike/uw-router.cjs.bak-pre-subpolicy-*` backup that is missing after a deploy was recorded, and on DETECTOR_DRIFT: `status.counters.main` grew while `sub` stayed 0 over at least 100 main requests in a session that has an Agent tool (the signal-rename failure mode, pre-mortem P3 and P9). OK line prints mode and age: `subagent policy: shadow, dynamic/same-provider/any, compiled 2026-10-03, 0 warnings, 1790 unverified allowed`.

Picker banner (S4): `policyNote` in `menu/pick-state.mjs` beside `feedNote` (`BENCH_FIELDS`, `:141`), set by `uwpick` from the compiled file and `status.json`; only problem states draw (EMPTY_SET, router unwired or silent, stale compile, shadow older than 14 days with no enforce decision, `shadow.flag` present, the last picker-exit auto-rebuild failed per `state/subagent/autorebuild.json`), so a healthy policy adds no clutter; and when a provider is picked as the new main, the picker shows a note if that provider is in the compiled `emptyProviders[]` OR, from S4b, in `noRowProviders` (revision 8 item 1; for a zero-row provider the wording is "no model of <provider> is allowed under mode <m> (<scope>)", plus "they will run unchanged" under `same-provider`) ("subagents on groq: no eligible model under mode free (free providers) / same-provider; they will run unchanged" (the scope is always named as `free models`, `free providers` or `free providers + deposit`, D-l, in this note, in the banner, in `set`, `show` and `explain`)). `emptyProviders[]` is required because `lists.byProvider` omits empty providers, so the picker could not tell "empty" from "not computed" (cr-M8). The pillar `uwpick-shows-latest-functional-state` is met by reading the files the router and `rebuild` refresh, not by a cached copy; a banner that cannot be refreshed (no status file) is omitted, not shown stale.

### 10.1 Toggle pane with live counts in `uwpick` (stage S4b, D-o)

What the owner asked: the toggle selector menu shows, in real time as toggles are selected, how many models are INCLUDED under the current combination, contingent on the other toggles. The design below is built on four verified constraints: (a) the picker static import graph may not reach `refresh/`, `keysync/` or `menu/catalog.mjs` (`test/bench-counts.test.mjs:253-275`); (b) first frame has a HARD 300 ms budget measured in a fresh process (`test/bench-startup.mjs`, `BUDGET_MS = 300`); (c) the bench is loaded lazily, once, when the first model screen is about to be drawn (`menu/uwpick.mjs:378-382`); (d) the frame floor is 78 columns (`menu/style.mjs:33`, `FRAME_MIN`).

1. ONE shared pure function (the requirement that CLI and picker counts can never disagree). `menu/subagent-funnel.mjs` (section 4) holds `funnel(inputs, toggles)` and every rule that changes a count; `keysync/subagent-policy.mjs` (the I/O shell behind `set`, `show`, `explain`, `rebuild` and the compiled file) and the pane BOTH import it, and `set`, `show` and the pane print the figures it returns under the same names (D-l). It lives in `menu/` because of constraint (a): `keysync/` is not importable from the picker, whereas `keysync/` already imports from `menu/` (for example `menu/atomic.mjs`). It is pure (no `fs`, no `node:` builtin, no clock: `now` is an input), so it is also what the parity test calls. The tier vocabulary it needs is `menu/tiers.mjs` (moved from `keysync/` for the same reason). The pane is loaded by DYNAMIC import on the first `ctrl+t`, so neither module is on the startup graph, the 300 ms budget and the import-graph test are unchanged, and a new assertion pins that `menu/uwpick.mjs` statically reaches neither `menu/subagent-funnel.mjs` nor `menu/subagent-pane.mjs`.
2. What the pane shows for the CURRENT toggle combination (all from `funnel`): models INCLUDED (total) and providers; the split verified (`v`) / small (`t`) / unverified (`u`) / ALIAS / not substitutable (unknown context or below the 128k floor); the count under each free scope (`free models`, `free providers`, `free providers + deposit`, always all three, D-l); EMPTY providers (`emptyProviders[]`, substitute pool 0), NO ROWS providers (`noRowProviders`, zero rows under the toggles, a row beside EMPTY with the same text as `set`; revision 8 item 1) and FRAGILE providers (`substitutable < 3`) with the SAME warning text as `set` (including the `FREE PROMISE` line and the plain helper-call statement of D-m); and the effective policy line (scope / source / ctx / enforcement / inject). "Same provider" counts use main's provider: the pane reads the router per-session files `state/subagent/main-<sid>.json` (`{model, beta1m, t}`, section 6.3) and takes the NEWEST one inside the 6 h TTL; when several sessions are inside the TTL with different providers the source row says `main: anthropic (newest of 3 sessions)` and key `p` cycles the assumed provider among those found; when no usable file exists the row says `main: unknown`, `same provider` shows a per-provider list (top 6 by count, then `+N`) and `p` lets the owner pick a provider. Whether the picker process inherits the launching session id is UNVERIFIED (E20), so the design never needs it.
3. Cost. A recompute is a filter over about 6,432 rows (milliseconds); it happens ONLY when a toggle changes, the pane opens or `r` reloads, never per frame, and is memoised per toggle tuple (at most 36 entries: 2 sources x 3 modes x 3 free scopes x 2 ctx; the owner text says "3 sources", the plan reads two, `same provider` and `all providers`, so 36 raw combinations of which 20 differ, because the free scope matters only under `free`). On open the pane builds one array of row records (selector, tier, ctx, flags) once; each recompute filters that array. MEASURED BUDGET with a test: median recompute under 20 ms and the open pre-pass under 50 ms on a synthetic 6,432-row snapshot fixture (the real row count), printing the numbers; CI asserts 5x those values to absorb noise.
4. Data inputs, all read lazily when the pane first opens and never at startup: the snapshot (already in memory); bench plus the observed overlay through the single `loadBench()` read the model screens use (reused if `meta.benchOf` is already loaded); the compiled `state/subagent/policy.json` for the provider-to-tier map (`tiers`), `compiledAt` and the owner block; the owner file `~/.llmkeys/subagent-policy.json` for the initial toggles (absent: source and mode show `(unset)` and Enter requires both); the `main-*.json` listing (at most 64 files). TIERS COME FROM THE REGISTRY-DERIVED `tiers` MAP IN THE COMPILED FILE, NEVER from the snapshot `keyId`, and the picker never parses `registry.json`, `providers.json` or any `keysync/` code. No shell-out, no `child_process`, no `node:sqlite`, no CCR call. If the compiled file is absent the scopes `free providers` and `free providers + deposit` show `n/a: run subagent-policy rebuild (tiers unknown)` and `free models` uses the strict fallback with the TIERS warning. STALENESS: the pane always prints "counts as of" with the snapshot `builtAt`, the bench `generatedAt` and the compiled `compiledAt`; it `stat`s `state/observed.json` on each keypress (one `statSync`) and, when the file is newer than the load, shows `STALE: live status moved since load (r reloads)` in the warning colour; `r` re-reads bench, overlay and compiled file and recomputes.
5. Interaction design. KEY: `ctrl+t` opens the pane from any level (chosen because it is free: not in `menu/legend.mjs:30-45`, not among the keys `menu/pick-state.mjs:576-583` documents as taken or terminal-reserved, ctrl+c/f/r/g/i/m/j/h/z/s/q/d, nor the four filters ctrl+o/l/x/e). The pane is a MODAL dispatched at the same pinned position as the refusals overlay (after ctrl+c), so while it is open it swallows every key and the live filter never sees them. Inside the pane: up/down choose a row, left/right or space change its value, and the shortcuts `s` source, `m` mode, `f` free scope (inert unless mode is `free`), `c` ctx, `p` main-provider assumption, `r` reload; `enter` saves, `esc` discards and closes, `?` opens the existing legend (which documents the pane: the `ctrl+t` row in the `KEYS` list of `menu/legend.mjs` plus a pane entry, a legend obligation with a test), `ctrl+c` quits as everywhere. LAYOUT at the 78-column floor (ASCII mode; illustrative figures; every line is exactly the frame width; at wider terminals each line pads to `g.width`; below `FRAME_MIN` the existing too-narrow notice applies; Unicode mode swaps `+-|` for the box glyphs, `>=` for the `ge` glyph and `...` for the ellipsis through the existing `g` glyph set):
```
+----------------------------------------------------------------------------+
| UW  subagent toggles    PREVIEW: enter saves, esc discards                 |
+----------------------------------------------------------------------------+
| source      same provider  [all providers]    main: anthropic (a1b2c3)     |
| mode        dynamic  inherit  [free]                                       |
| free scope  free models  [free providers]  free providers + deposit        |
| ctx         [any]  >= 1M                                                   |
+----------------------------------------------------------------------------+
| INCLUDED NOW  1,687 models on 46 providers      counts as of 10-03 09:49   |
|  verified 0  small 0  unverified 1,687  ALIAS 6  not substitutable 860     |
|  free models 112  free providers 1,687  free providers + deposit 1,687     |
| EMPTY  fx-free-b (+3)                  FRAGILE  fx-dep fx-paid (<3 usable) |
| policy  free providers / all providers / any ctx / shadow / inject off     |
| WARN    helper calls (titles, summaries, compaction) still use the relay   |
| AUTO-APPLY  closing the picker after a saved change automatically runs     |
|             subagent-policy rebuild --if-stale yes --quiet yes (permanent) |
+----------------------------------------------------------------------------+
| s source  m mode  f free scope  c ctx  r reload  enter save  esc undo      |
+----------------------------------------------------------------------------+
```
Colour bands reuse the palette helpers the existing `free` and `ok` columns use (no new palette entries): the included total in the `ok` band, FRAGILE and STALE in the warning band, EMPTY and a refused Enter in the error band; with no colour the same words appear and bracket marks show the selected value. PREVIEW-ONLY SEMANTICS: nothing is written while browsing. `enter` first applies the shared refusal rule to the current figures (substitute pool empty for every provider, or for main's provider under `same provider`: refused inside the pane with the `E_EMPTY` text, no write; there is no `--allow-empty` in the pane, that stays CLI); otherwise it writes the owner file through the SAME merge and serializer as the CLI `set` (`mergeOwner` and `serializeOwner`, exported by `menu/subagent-funnel.mjs`) and `menu/atomic.mjs` `writeAtomic` (which `key.mjs` also uses); only source, mode, free scope and ctx change, and `enforcement`, `inject`, `unverified`, `classLog` and `allow` are preserved (first save: `shadow`, `off`, `allow-warn`, `on`, empty). `esc` discards. The pane never restarts anything, never calls CCR and never spawns a process. APPLYING A SAVE (D-p, owner decision of revision 6, replaces the earlier "by default only if Q4 is yes" wording): the router reads only the COMPILED file, so a saved owner file is not live until it is compiled; the picker cannot compile (import graph, no shell-out), so the existing wrapper `menu/uwpick-run.ps1` hashes the owner file before it starts the picker and, AFTER the picker process exits, runs `node keysync/key.mjs subagent-policy rebuild --if-stale yes --quiet yes` ONLY IF the hash changed (same bytes before and after: no rebuild, no process spawned; a changed hash: exactly one run). It is non-fatal and quiet, bounded by a timeout, a read-only `getConfig` only, outside the picker import graph, and it can never enable enforcement or restart the gateway (it only compiles). A failure writes `state/subagent/autorebuild.json` and the NEXT picker open shows `auto-rebuild failed (exit N, time): run subagent-policy rebuild` (banner and pane); until a rebuild has succeeded, the picker banner (S4) and the doctor say `policy not applied: owner file newer than compiled`. PERMANENT WARNING (D-p condition a): the pane ALWAYS draws the two AUTO-APPLY rows of the sketch (not dismissible, no key hides them, shown on every open whatever the toggles or the save state) and the legend/help carries the same sentence; the required phrase is pinned by test (9.4 item 14). SAVE TEXT: after `enter` the pane says `saved; closing the picker will run subagent-policy rebuild automatically (or run it now: node keysync/key.mjs subagent-policy rebuild)`, and `esc` after no save says nothing about rebuilding beyond the permanent rows; the CLI step remains available for an owner who does not want to wait for the exit. SHADOW VERSUS ENFORCE: the pane shows the router mode in the policy line and in a banner row (`shadow: logging only, counts show what WOULD be served` or `ENFORCING: a save changes live routing once compiled`), but it has NO mode toggle: moving to `enforce` is a gated step (G3: classifier PASS, fit fields, `providersLive`), `set --enforce enforce` refuses when a precondition is unmet, and the pane cannot check those without compile inputs, so one keypress in a picker must never be able to move live routing; that stays a CLI act.
6. Pane counts versus what the router will serve (R-pane-1). The pane shows policy ELIGIBILITY, not a promise of service: it applies the resolvability filter with the snapshot `routable` flags (the `routableOf` the picker already uses, `menu/uwpick.mjs:264-285`), whereas the compile applies LIVE `Providers` when a providers summary is available and the router applies `config.Providers` per request. The pane therefore labels its figure "eligible" and, when a compiled file exists for the SAME toggle tuple and its `counts.allowed` differs from the pane figure, adds `compiled says N (as of T)` in the warning band, which exposes live-Providers or overlay drift instead of hiding it.
7. Tests are in 9.4; the stage is S4b in 12.1; risks R-pane-1..4 and pre-mortem P13, P14 are in 12.4.

### 10.2 Warning paths, HUD, and which model ran

HUD: out of scope (see non-goals). WHICH warning paths are instant, and which are NOT (cr-M8; revision 1 said "instant" for all of them):
- INSTANT: (1) the `set` refusal and warning list, at configuration time; (2) the picker note at pick time: `uwpick` writes the `/model` command at `menu/uwpick.mjs:267` (`finish` -> `modelCommand`) and `pick-state` reads the compiled file when the picker draws, so the note appears in the same interaction in which main's provider changes.
- NOT instant, and said so: (3) the native `/model` command typed inside Claude Code changes main's model without passing through `uwpick` or any UW code; this feature does not cover it (documented in the runbook; the router still learns the new main from the next request, and runtime emptiness then shows only via the paths below); (4) RUNTIME emptiness (main switched provider, set emptied later) reaches the owner only through `status.json`, the once-per-session `EMPTY_SET` decision-log line, `show`, the doctor and the banner, and reaches main (hence the user) through the injected warning only when injection is on (default off); (5) DATA-DRIVEN emptiness after a bench sweep or keysync run exists only after `subagent-policy rebuild` is run (manually from S2, automatically from S4), because the compiled file is a snapshot of the inputs; until then the compiled file and the picker note describe the old data (the doctor WARNs when the compiled file is older than the snapshot or the bench).

Which model each subagent ran on. After the fact: `decisions.jsonl` (`asked`, `tag`, `would`, `ret`, `act`, per session prefix) and CCR's `request_logs.requested_model` / `resolved_model` / `response_model` plus the `x-ccr-routed-model` header the trace shows being added (F4). Whether `requested_model` records the pre-router or post-router value is checked in S3 (today the router echoes `asked`, so the live rows cannot tell). At spawn time the model is not visible to the user, only to main via the injected list. What is NOT visible anywhere: CCR rewrites `message_start.model` on streams, so Claude Code's own transcript and `/status` show the REQUESTED model, never the served one; the true served model exists only in CCR's request log and in our decision log. Say this in the runbook so nobody trusts the transcript (recorded subagent model is the requested one in Claude Code, anthropics/claude-code#85592).

### 10.3 The `TOOLS` column and its legend in `uwpick` (revision 10; stage S5c; owner decisions D-aa, D-ab; CORRECTED the same day: the ladder is section 11's)

CORRECTION. An earlier text of this section used level wording that was NOT section 11's (it was an invention of the delivery that carried it). Section 11 (issue #121) is CANONICAL: this section uses its REAL ladder, its record fields and its `t` derivation, unchanged. Nothing below renames or loosens anything in section 11.

Purpose (owner): after the tool-capability probe sweep, the picker shows the tool-capability result per probe-ok model in the model-level menu, at a glance. It shows what the model ACTUALLY did under a tool-bearing request, which the catalogue `tools` claim does not (section 11).

A. THE LADDER (section 11, unchanged): L1 one forced tool call that returns a well-formed `tool_use` with valid JSON arguments; L2 a `tool_result` round trip that yields a final answer with no 400; L3 a versioned, redacted, SYNTHETIC fixture of realistic Claude Code tool schemas (about a 157 KB request, `fixtureId`, "verified at N KB"); L4 two parallel tool calls with streamed arguments. L1 and L2 always run together as the first pass (D-i); L3 and L4 run lazily.
B. THE DATA (section 11's store, unchanged): `state/tool-fidelity.json`, its own file, never in the snapshot, no schema bump anywhere. Stored record per `benchKey`: `{lv, why, at, fx, ok, maxBytes, alias}` exactly as section 11 defines them (`lv` 0 none, 1 L1 and L2 passed, 3 L3 passed, 4 L4 passed; `ok` the boolean verdict; `why` the redacted failure reason; `at` ISO time; `fx` the `fixtureId`; `maxBytes` the size L3 sent; `alias` advisory for pool aliases). The compact class `t` keeps section 11's derivation, NOT loosened: `v` = L3 passed (or known good by provenance, the Anthropic relay), `t` = L1 and L2 passed (L3 not run or not passed), `x` = failed, `u` = not probed, or an ALIAS or pool row (D-n); NEVER `u` because of age (D-ap: the class always comes from the LAST recorded result, and an outdated fixture version `fx` only adds the `*` marker in the picker).
